# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-26)

## Corpus Check
- 5586 files · ~13,140,501 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 295 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .log 178, (none) 34, .css 16)

## Summary
- 69706 nodes · 206305 edges · 1425 communities (1294 shown, 131 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 8576 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `16984b13`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
